- **perf-baseline-check**: Re-run the §13 performance budgets (build size, choice p95, save p95, save bytes, heap, FPS, soak heap drift) and diff results against game/docs/perf-baseline.md. Honors the operator-supplied tolerance, reports per-dimension deltas, and classifies the overall verdict as GREEN / YELLOW / RED. Pairs with perf-baseline-record. - **perf-change**: Use when optimizing performance-sensitive code, when asked to make something faster, or before claiming a speedup for a change.
